**design_boundary:** SDD20: semantic claims retain provenance/confidence/history; structured exact retrieval first. Notes do not grant admission, override Risk, or establish current economic truth.

**costs_removal:** 1746 tracked bytes; no standalone CPU/retention schedule. Bootstrap duplicates source once by design; removal severs note identity/links and historical context; no source deletion authorized by age or static usage.

**inspection:** Entire file read; exact metadata/body and cross-file consumers checked. Original note/evidence identity retained.

No retries, queue, new provider use or retained memory introduced. Per-record/query bounds are enforced by owner queries; step count does not independently cap all reply bytes/tool-call cardinality. Simplification closes dispatch divergence and removes a redundant registry dispatch branch.

**inspection:** Full source and concrete consumer/test bodies inspected offline; baseline 4deca5c275c30c2c59c227b5170bab651cf777fe retained; new regression baseline counterexamples captured.

**costs_removal:** At most one synchronous request/send, 10s timeout, no retry or durable queue; unconfigured returns False. API acknowledgement proves accepted message, not owner receipt or current system state. Caller message correctness remains producer responsibility. Delivery/HTML failures return False; no new alert ledger, outbox, retries or polling replacement added.

**inspection:** Full source and concrete consumer/test bodies inspected offline; baseline 4deca5c275c30c2c59c227b5170bab651cf777fe retained; new regression baseline counterexamples captured.
